Package size varies or is ambiguous, so auto-fill is intentionally disabled.`;%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ // GLYMIZE_MARKET_V23_INTEGRATION%0A+ export default function Type2ScenariosClient() {%0A+   const { locale, isRtl } = useGlymizeLocale();%0A+   const fa = locale === "fa";%0A+   const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<GenericMedication[]>([]);%0A+   const [currentHba1c, setCurrentHba1c] = useState("");%0A+   const [targetHba1c, setTargetHba1c] = useState("7");%0A+   const [medications, setMedications] = useState<Type2MedicationRow[]>([]);%0A+   const [factors, setFactors] = useState<Type2DecisionFactor[]>([]);%0A+   const [worldDrugDomains, setWorldDrugDomains] = useState<MedicationClinicalDomain[]>([]);%0A+   const [context, setContext] = useState<Type2CoreContextDraft>({ ...emptyType2CoreContextDraft });%0A+   const [structuredContext, setStructuredContext] = useState<Type2StructuredIntakeDraft>({ ...emptyType2StructuredIntakeDraft });%0A+   const [costPreference, setCostPreference] = useState<Type2CostPreference>("moderate");%0A+   const [routePreference, setRoutePreference] = useState<Type2RoutePreference>("oral_and_injectable");%0A+   const [insuranceProvider, setInsuranceProvider] = useState<InsuranceProvider>("social_security");%0A+   const [scenarioSortMode, setScenarioSortMode] = useState<Type2ScenarioSortMode>("balanced");%0A+   const [hyperglycemiaSymptoms, setHyperglycemiaSymptoms] = useState(false);%0A+   const [catabolicFeatures, setCatabolicFeatures] = useState(false);%0A+   const [assessment, setAssessment] = useState<Type2AssessmentWithParallelSafety | null>(null);%0A+   const [submittedRequest, setSubmittedRequest] = useState<Type2ConsiderationRequest | null>(null);%0A+   const [costPlans, setCostPlans] = useState<Record<string, Type2CostingPlan>>({});%0A+   const [status, setStatus] = useState("");%0A+%0A+   useEffect(() => {%0A+     void apiFetch("/v1/catalog/generics")%0A+       .then((response) => response.ok ? function toman(value?: number, locale: "fa" | "en" = "fa") {%0A+   if (value === undefined) return "—";%0A+   return new Intl.NumberFormat(locale === "fa" ? "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase();%0A+   if (normalized === "insulin_unit" || normalized === "unit" || normalized === "u") return "unit";%0A+   if (normalized === "ml") return "ml";%0A+   return normalized;%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ function currentDailyQuantityForProfile(%0A+   profile: ClinicianMedicationCostingProfile | undefined,%0A+   medicationId: string,%0A+   genericName: string,%0A+   request: Type2ConsiderationRequest,%0A+ ) {%0A+   if (!profile) return undefined;%0A+   const current = (request.currentMedications ?? "").trim().toLocaleLowerCase();%0A+   if (normalized === "insulin_unit" || normalized === "unit" || normalized === "u") return "unit";%0A+   if (normalized === "ml") return "ml";%0A+   return normalized;%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ function currentDailyQuantityForProfile(%0A+   profile: ClinicianMedicationCostingProfile | undefined,%0A+   medicationId: string,%0A+   genericName: string,%0A+   request: Type2ConsiderationRequest,%0A+ ) {%0A+   if (!profile) return undefined;%0A+   const current = (request.currentMedications ?? []).find((item) =>%0A+     item.genericMedicationId === medicationId ||%0A+     item.genericName.trim().toLocaleLowerCase() === genericName.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()%0A+   );%0A+   if (!current || typeof current.totalDailyDose !== "number" || current.totalDailyDose <= 0) return undefined;%0A+   const expected = normalizedCostUnit(profile.dailyInputUnit);%0A+   const actual = normalizedCostUnit(current.totalDailyDoseUnit ?? current.doseUnit);%0A+   return expected === actual ? current.totalDailyDose : undefined;%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ function costingProfileHint(%0A+   profile: ClinicianMedicationCostingProfile | undefined,%0A+   fa: boolean,%0A+ ) {%0A+   if (!profile) return fa%0A+     ? "Package profile معتبر برای این دارو ثبت نشده است؛ مقدار بسته را پزشک وارد می‌کند."%0A+     : "No validated package profile is available; enter the package measure manually.";%0A+%0A+   const labels = costingUnitLabels(profile, fa);%0A+   if (%0A+     profile.autoFillEligible &&%0A+     profile.packageMeasureQuantity !== undefined &&%0A+     profile.displayContainerCount !== undefined &&%0A+     profile.displayQuantityPerContainer !== undefined%0A+   ) {%0A+     return fa%0A+       ? `بسته NFI: ${profile.displayContainerCount} ${profile.displayContainerUnit ?? Package size varies or is ambiguous, so auto-fill is intentionally disabled.`;%0A+ }%0A+%0A+ // GLYMIZE_MARKET_V23_INTEGRATION%0A+ export default function Type2ScenariosClient() {%0A+   const { locale, isRtl } = useGlymizeLocale();%0A+   const fa = locale === "fa";%0A+   const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<GenericMedication[]>([]);%0A+   const [currentHba1c, setCurrentHba1c] = useState("");%0A+   const [targetHba1c, setTargetHba1c] = useState("7");%0A+   const [medications, setMedications] = useState<Type2MedicationRow[]>([]);%0A+   const [factors, setFactors] = useState<Type2DecisionFactor[]>([]);%0A+   const [worldDrugDomains, setWorldDrugDomains] = useState<MedicationClinicalDomain[]>([]);%0A+   const [context, setContext] = useState<Type2CoreContextDraft>({ ...emptyType2CoreContextDraft });%0A+   const [structuredContext, setStructuredContext] = useState<Type2StructuredIntakeDraft>({ ...emptyType2StructuredIntakeDraft });%0A+   const [costPreference, setCostPreference] = useState<Type2CostPreference>("moderate");%0A+   const [routePreference, setRoutePreference] = useState<Type2RoutePreference>("oral_and_injectable");%0A+   const [insuranceProvider, setInsuranceProvider] = useState<InsuranceProvider>("social_security");%0A+   const [scenarioSortMode, setScenarioSortMode] = useState<Type2ScenarioSortMode>("balanced");%0A+   const [hyperglycemiaSymptoms, setHyperglycemiaSymptoms] = useState(false);%0A+   const [catabolicFeatures, setCatabolicFeatures] = useState(false);%0A+   const [assessment, setAssessment] = useState<Type2AssessmentWithParallelSafety | null>(null);%0A+   const [submittedRequest, setSubmittedRequest] = useState<Type2ConsiderationRequest | null>(null);%0A+   const [costPlans, setCostPlans] = useState<Record<string, Type2CostingPlan>>({});%0A+   const [status, setStatus] = useState("");%0A+%0A+   useEffect(() => {%0A+     void apiFetch("/v1/catalog/generics")%0A+       .then((response) => response.ok ?
